Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any tokenization module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 16. The projective resolution has length at most 817 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: By the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_16(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 16. The extension on the ambient space provides the required map, and the obstruction class in H^817(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: In the tokenization framework, every structural decomposition morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 695. The resulting operator achieves bound O(305/n^5).

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to tokenization theory, we decompose the problem into 695 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 305. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(5/n). QED.
